# P2b.2b.2c.3b push map, plan Task C4: the checks and each roll's summary on the Amendment C record (stage 2)
#
# Record: reference/derived/envelope-<type>.csv.gz and envelope-defaults.json (commit 3d9ac00; engine 0.30.0, run
# commit 0386a1f5eec94e34272a2d04d3201906471278be). Runs: drive 42514733880; AC stop 42514735843, 42587166487,
# 42772693923; half roll 42514737914; single ball 42514740002; full roll 42514742137, 42514743927; pass roll
# 42514745719, 42772697891, 42772701429, 42888967334 (https://github.com/Jodre11/croquet-sim/actions/runs/<id>).
# The runs' shard files downloaded on 2026-11-11 and merged oldest first, each job's latest upload winning (B-d12).
#
# The checks: env SECTION=checks SHARDS=<merged shard files> npx --no-install tsx scripts/pushMap.ts
# Each roll's summary: env SECTION=summary TYPE=<roll> IN=<merged shard files> npx --no-install tsx scripts/pushMap.ts
#
# Impulse-variability scaling (design §6 item 4). Schmidt, Zelaznik, Hawkins, Frank and Quinn 1979, "Motor-output
# variability: A theory for the accuracy of rapid motor acts", Psychological Review 86(5), 415-451 (PMID 504536). The
# full text could not be fetched (ResearchGate and APA returned 403 on 2026-11-11); its Europe PMC record holds no
# abstract. The theory is read from an open review that states it:
#   Plamondon and Alimi 1997, "Speed/accuracy trade-offs in target-directed movements", Behavioral and Brain Sciences
#   20, 279-349. https://www.cse.lehigh.edu/prr/Biometrics/Archive/Papers/SpeedAccuracy.pdf, fetched 2026-11-11 with
#   curl, SHA-256 907e03706dc470fa3843146cffa4a4779b6d8f248b1b5948c3b2f620c9af64bd.
#   Quotation: "proportionality relationships – the first between the impulse and the average velocity". Tells the
#   model: a rapid act's impulse scales with the speed it produces, the force-time shape kept. Does not tell it: the
#   croquet stroke's push shape, its duration, or whether a player keeps one shape across ranges.
# Read against the defaults below: along range each band's default changes its follow-through shape 5 to 8 times
# and its push impulse per unit power varies several-fold, so the defaults are not one shape scaled; recorded, not
# fitted away. The push windows chosen, 10-40 ms, sit at and below the CA's measured roll contacts, 30-58 ms.
#
# The hole between the drive and the AC stop (ratios 3.6-4.2 at the striker's 1.3-6.4 m): at every one the drive's
# neighbouring grid leans, 0° and +6°, land either side of it (on the 10 s lawn at 3.32 m, +6° gives 3.52-3.59 and
# 0° gives 4.33-4.42). The hole is the drive's 6° lean step, not the physics. The other inside holes lie at the AC
# stop's steep edge (ratios above 6).
